\displaystyle\tilde{C}(\alpha,\beta,d,\Theta)=\sup_{\tilde{\lambda}}\left[(1+||\tilde{\lambda}||_{2}^{2})^{\alpha+\frac{d}{2}-\beta}|\hat{K}(\tilde{\lambda})|^{2}\right]\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\beta}^{2}<\infty

because
K
is a higher-order kernel. Consequently, by setting
h=\delta^{1/\beta}
and noting that this implies
||f_{\theta,h}-w_{\theta}||_{\infty}<\delta
, I obtain that there is a constant
\acute{C}(\alpha,\beta,d,\Theta)>0
such that

\displaystyle\inf_{f\in\mathcal{H}:||f-w_{\theta}||_{\infty}<\delta}\frac{1}{2}||f||_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq\acute{C}(\alpha,\beta,d,\Theta)\delta^{-(2\alpha+d-2\beta)/\beta}\quad\forall\ \theta\in\Theta,

which implies Part 1 of the lemma.

Step 3B.

Suppose that
\alpha<\beta<\alpha+d/2
. Since
\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\alpha}<\infty
if
\alpha<\beta
and
\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\beta}<\infty
, a similar argument to Step 3A reveals that

\displaystyle||f_{\theta,h}||_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq\bar{C}(d,h)\tilde{C}(d,\alpha,\Theta)\leq\acute{C}(\alpha,d,\Theta)h^{-d},

for
0<h\leq 1
, where

\displaystyle\bar{C}(d,h)=\sup_{\lambda}\left(\frac{1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2}}{1+||h\lambda||_{2}^{2}}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}}.

and

\displaystyle\tilde{C}(d,\alpha,\Theta)\sup_{\lambda}\left[(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\frac{d}{2}}|\hat{K}(\lambda)|^{2}\right]\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\alpha}^{2}

Setting
h=\delta^{1/\beta}
, it similarly follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\inf_{f\in\mathcal{H}:||f-w_{\theta}||_{\infty}<\delta}\frac{1}{2}||f||_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq\acute{C}(\alpha,d,\Theta)\delta^{-\frac{d}{\beta}}.

Step 3C.

Suppose that
\beta\geq\alpha+d/2
. To show that
\{w_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\}\subseteq\mathcal{H}
, it suffices, by Lemma 11.35 of
Ghosal and Van der Vaart (2017)
, that the following holds:

\displaystyle\int|\hat{w}_{\theta}(\lambda)|^{2}(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\alpha+\frac{d}{2}}d\lambda<\infty

for all
\theta\in\Theta
. Since
\beta\geq\alpha+d/2
implies that

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda}\left(\frac{(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\alpha+\frac{d}{2}}}{(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\beta}}\right)\leq 1,

it follows, by Hölder’s inequality, that

\displaystyle\int|\hat{w}_{\theta}(\lambda)|^{2}(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\alpha+\frac{d}{2}}d\lambda\leq\int|\hat{w}_{\theta}(\lambda)|^{2}(1+||\lambda||_{2}^{2})^{\beta}d\lambda\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\beta}^{2}<\infty

for all
\theta\in\Theta
by the assumption on
\{w_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\}
. This establishes that
\{w_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\}\subseteq\mathcal{H}
, and, since, for any
\delta>0
,
\{w_{\theta}:\theta\in\Theta\}\subseteq\{f\in\mathcal{H}:||f-w_{\theta}||_{\infty}<\delta\}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\inf_{f:||f-w_{\theta}||_{\infty}<\delta}\frac{1}{2}||f||_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\leq\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}||w_{\theta}||_{2,2,\beta}^{2}<\infty.

∎

Discussion of the Matérn Example.

Given a formal statement of Lemma 13, I elaborate on the rates for the Matérn GP:

1.

RMSH Rate:
Lemma 3 of
van der Vaart and van Zanten (2011)
states that
\tilde{\Pi}_{\mathcal{B}}
satisfies
-\log\tilde{\Pi}_{\mathcal{B}}(||B||_{\infty}<\delta)\leq C\delta^{-d/\alpha}
for any
\delta>0
, where
C>0
is a constant that does not depend on
\delta
. Using this and Lemma 13, a sequence
\delta_{n}
that satisfies
\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\varphi_{b_{0,\theta}}(\delta_{n})\lesssim n\delta_{n}^{2}
can be computed as follows:

(a)

Solve
\delta_{n}^{-(2\alpha+d-2\alpha_{0})/\alpha_{0}}\lesssim n\delta_{n}^{2}
and
\delta_{n}^{-\frac{d}{\alpha}}\lesssim n\delta_{n}^{2}
if
\alpha_{0}\leq\alpha
.

(b)

Solve
\delta_{n}^{-\frac{d}{\alpha}}\lesssim n\delta_{n}^{2}
if
\alpha_{0}>\alpha
.

Case (a) is satisfied for
\delta_{n}=n^{-\alpha_{0}/(2\alpha+d)}=n^{-\min\{\alpha,\alpha_{0}\}/(2\alpha+d)}
, while Case (b) is satisfied for
\delta_{n}=n^{-\alpha/(2\alpha+d)}=n^{-\min\{\alpha,\alpha_{0}\}/(2\alpha+d)}
. Rearranging
\min\{\alpha_{0},\alpha\}/(2\alpha+d)>1/4
yields
\alpha_{0}>\alpha/2+d/4
and
\alpha>d/2
as conditions for
o(n^{-1/4})
.

2.

Maximum Hellinger Rate:
The leading term for the sequence
\tilde{\delta}_{n}
derived in Proposition 2 is
2^{J(\frac{d_{z}}{2}-\alpha_{0})}+\sqrt{n}\delta_{n}2^{J(\frac{d_{z}}{2}-a)}
. Consequently, plugging in
\delta_{n}=n^{-\min\{\alpha,\alpha_{0}\}/(2\alpha+d)}
and
2^{J}=(n/\log n)^{1/(2\alpha+d_{z})}
leads to

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\delta_{n}2^{J_{n}(\frac{d_{z}}{2}-a)}+2^{J_{n}(\frac{d_{z}}{2}-\alpha_{0})}=n^{\frac{(\alpha-a)+d_{z}}{2\alpha+d_{z}}-\frac{\min\{\alpha,\alpha_{0}\}}{(2\alpha+d)}}(\log n)^{c_{1}}+n^{\frac{(d_{z}-2\alpha_{0})}{2(2\alpha+d_{z})}}(\log n)^{c_{2}},

where
c_{1},c_{2}>0
. Consequently, the condition
\tilde{\delta}_{n}=o(n^{-1/4})
holds if

\displaystyle\frac{\min\{\alpha,\alpha_{0}\}}{2\alpha+d}-\frac{(\alpha-a)+d_{z}}{2\alpha+d_{z}}>\frac{1}{4},\quad\frac{2\alpha_{0}-d_{z}}{2(2\alpha+d_{z})}>1/4,

which, when rearranged, lead to the conditions in the maim text. Figure
1
plots feasible
(\alpha,\alpha_{0})
pairs for different values of
(d_{w},d_{z})
, highlighting the
o(n^{-1/4})
condition is met if there is enough smoothness, and the prior and true smoothness are not too different.

3.

Prior Invariance:
Applying Lemma 13,
\zeta_{n,j}=n^{-\beta_{0,j}/(2\alpha+d)}
if
\beta_{0,j}\leq\alpha
and
\zeta_{n,j}=n^{-\beta_{0,j}/(2\beta_{0,j}+d)}
if
\beta_{0,j}\in(\alpha,\alpha+d/2)
. Consequently, if
\underline{\beta}:=\min_{1\leq j\leq d_{\gamma}}\beta_{0,j}\leq\alpha
, then
\max_{1\leq j\leq d_{\gamma}}\zeta_{n,j}=o(n^{-1/4})
if
\underline{\beta}/(2\alpha+d)>1/4
, which is the same as
\underline{\beta}>\alpha/2+d/4
. If
\underline{\beta}\in(\alpha,\alpha+d/2)
, then
\max_{1\leq j\leq d_{\gamma}}\zeta_{n,j}=o(n^{-1/4})
iff
\beta_{0,j}>d/2
for all
j=1,...,d_{\gamma}
, which is true if
\alpha>d/2
. If
\underline{\beta}\geq\alpha+d/2
, then the conditions of Proposition 3 hold automatically.

Figure 1.
Feasible
(\alpha,\alpha_{0})
Pairs for Empirical Uniform Convergence
